     * This method holds STRUCTURE only: which keys to look for, which are required, what to pass

     * OpenAPI field constraints, the wording of an error — stays in the deserializer and arrives
     * through `$cast`. That is deliberate: the general loop and this method cannot answer
     * differently about a value, because only one of them has an opinion.
     *
     * Emitted only for a schema this shape covers: every property a plain body field, no parameter
     * source, no default, no `readOnly`, no inheritance, no discriminator.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data
     * @param Closure(string, mixed, array<int, string>): mixed $cast casts, and appends any problem to $errors
     * @param array<int, string> $errors

    public static function hydrateFast(array $data, Closure $cast, array &$errors): self
    {
        $fileGiven = array_key_exists('file', $data);
        if (!$fileGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "file" not found in request.';
        }
        $file = $fileGiven ? $cast('file', $data['file'], $errors) : null;

        if ($errors !== []) {
            throw new RuntimeException(implode("\n", array_unique(array_filter($errors))));
        }

        /** @var UploadedFile $file */
        $dto = new self(
            file: $file,
        );

        // No presence flags to raise here: the constructor already derives each one from whether its
        // argument is the sentinel, and for every property this method passes exactly what the
        // caller sent. Assigning them again was dead code in every generated class.
        return $dto;
    }

    /**
     * Straight-line hydration from a decoded body — machinery for `DtoDeserializer`, not an API to
     * call yourself.
     *
     * This method holds STRUCTURE only: which keys to look for, which are required, what to pass
     * the constructor, which presence flags to raise. Every decision ABOUT A VALUE — casting, the
     * OpenAPI field constraints, the wording of an error — stays in the deserializer and arrives
     * through `$cast`. That is deliberate: the general loop and this method cannot answer
     * differently about a value, because only one of them has an opinion.
     *
     * Emitted only for a schema this shape covers: every property a plain body field, no parameter
     * source, no default, no `readOnly`, no inheritance, no discriminator.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data
     * @param Closure(string, mixed, array<int, string>): mixed $cast casts, and appends any problem to $errors
     * @param array<int, string> $errors
     */
    public static function hydrateFast(array $data, Closure $cast, array &$errors): self
    {
        $mustContainGiven = array_key_exists('mustContain', $data);
        if (!$mustContainGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "mustContain" not found in request.';
        }
        $mustContain = $mustContainGiven ? $cast('mustContain', $data['mustContain'], $errors) : null;
        $positionsGiven = array_key_exists('positions', $data);
        $positions = $positionsGiven ? $cast('positions', $data['positions'], $errors) : null;
        $restGiven = array_key_exists('rest', $data);
        $rest = $restGiven ? $cast('rest', $data['rest'], $errors) : null;
        $conditionalGiven = array_key_exists('conditional', $data);
        $conditional = $conditionalGiven ? $cast('conditional', $data['conditional'], $errors) : null;
        $keyedGiven = array_key_exists('keyed', $data);
        $keyed = $keyedGiven ? $cast('keyed', $data['keyed'], $errors) : null;

        if ($errors !== []) {
            throw new RuntimeException(implode("\n", array_unique(array_filter($errors))));
        }

        /** @var array $mustContain */
        $dto = new self(
            mustContain: $mustContain,
            positions: $positionsGiven ? $positions : UnsetValue::UNSET,
            rest: $restGiven ? $rest : UnsetValue::UNSET,
            conditional: $conditionalGiven ? $conditional : UnsetValue::UNSET,
            keyed: $keyedGiven ? $keyed : UnsetValue::UNSET,
        );

        // No presence flags to raise here: the constructor already derives each one from whether its
        // argument is the sentinel, and for every property this method passes exactly what the
        // caller sent. Assigning them again was dead code in every generated class.
        return $dto;
    }

                'readOnly' => false,
                'isParameter' => false,
                'arrayItemType' => '',
            ],
        ];

    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    public static function getAliases(): array
    {
        return [];
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public static function getConstraints(): array
    {
        $constraints = [];
        $constraints['trigger'] = ['type' => 'string'];

        return $constraints;
    }

    /**
     * Schema-LEVEL keywords, as opposed to the per-property map in `getConstraints()`.
     *
     * Emitted only when the document sets one the runtime can act on — a class without it simply
     * has nothing to enforce, and the deserializer reads an absent method as exactly that.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public static function getObjectConstraints(): array
    {
        $constraints = [];
        $constraints['dependentSchemas'] = ['trigger' => ['type' => 'object', 'required' => ['label'], 'properties' => ['label' => ['type' => 'string', 'minLength' => 2]]]];

        return $constraints;
    }

    /**
     * Straight-line hydration from a decoded body — machinery for `DtoDeserializer`, not an API to
     * call yourself.
     *
     * This method holds STRUCTURE only: which keys to look for, which are required, what to pass
     * the constructor, which presence flags to raise. Every decision ABOUT A VALUE — casting, the
     * OpenAPI field constraints, the wording of an error — stays in the deserializer and arrives
     * through `$cast`. That is deliberate: the general loop and this method cannot answer
     * differently about a value, because only one of them has an opinion.
     *
     * Emitted only for a schema this shape covers: every property a plain body field, no parameter
     * source, no default, no `readOnly`, no inheritance, no discriminator.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data
     * @param Closure(string, mixed, array<int, string>): mixed $cast casts, and appends any problem to $errors
     * @param array<int, string> $errors
     */
    public static function hydrateFast(array $data, Closure $cast, array &$errors): self
    {
        $triggerGiven = array_key_exists('trigger', $data);
        $trigger = $triggerGiven ? $cast('trigger', $data['trigger'], $errors) : null;

        if ($errors !== []) {
            throw new RuntimeException(implode("\n", array_unique(array_filter($errors))));
        }

        $dto = new self(
            trigger: $triggerGiven ? $trigger : UnsetValue::UNSET,
        );

        // No presence flags to raise here: the constructor already derives each one from whether its
        // argument is the sentinel, and for every property this method passes exactly what the
        // caller sent. Assigning them again was dead code in every generated class.
        return $dto;
    }

    /**
     * Straight-line hydration from a decoded body — machinery for `DtoDeserializer`, not an API to
     * call yourself.
     *
     * This method holds STRUCTURE only: which keys to look for, which are required, what to pass
     * the constructor, which presence flags to raise. Every decision ABOUT A VALUE — casting, the
     * OpenAPI field constraints, the wording of an error — stays in the deserializer and arrives
     * through `$cast`. That is deliberate: the general loop and this method cannot answer
     * differently about a value, because only one of them has an opinion.
     *
     * Emitted only for a schema this shape covers: every property a plain body field, no parameter
     * source, no default, no `readOnly`, no inheritance, no discriminator.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data
     * @param Closure(string, mixed, array<int, string>): mixed $cast casts, and appends any problem to $errors
     * @param array<int, string> $errors
     */
    public static function hydrateFast(array $data, Closure $cast, array &$errors): self
    {
        $listOfListsGiven = array_key_exists('listOfLists', $data);
        if (!$listOfListsGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "listOfLists" not found in request.';
        }
        $listOfLists = $listOfListsGiven ? $cast('listOfLists', $data['listOfLists'], $errors) : null;
        $listOfMapsGiven = array_key_exists('listOfMaps', $data);
        $listOfMaps = $listOfMapsGiven ? $cast('listOfMaps', $data['listOfMaps'], $errors) : null;
        $mapOfListsGiven = array_key_exists('mapOfLists', $data);
        $mapOfLists = $mapOfListsGiven ? $cast('mapOfLists', $data['mapOfLists'], $errors) : null;
        $plainGiven = array_key_exists('plain', $data);
        $plain = $plainGiven ? $cast('plain', $data['plain'], $errors) : null;

        if ($errors !== []) {
            throw new RuntimeException(implode("\n", array_unique(array_filter($errors))));
        }

        /** @var array $listOfLists */
        $dto = new self(
            listOfLists: $listOfLists,
            listOfMaps: $listOfMapsGiven ? $listOfMaps : UnsetValue::UNSET,
            mapOfLists: $mapOfListsGiven ? $mapOfLists : UnsetValue::UNSET,
            plain: $plainGiven ? $plain : UnsetValue::UNSET,
        );

        // No presence flags to raise here: the constructor already derives each one from whether its
        // argument is the sentinel, and for every property this method passes exactly what the
        // caller sent. Assigning them again was dead code in every generated class.
        return $dto;
    }

     * @return array<string, array<string, mixed>>
     */

    {

        $constraints['dates'] = ['type' => 'array', 'items' => ['type' => 'string', 'format' => 'date']];
        $constraints['datesByDay'] = ['type' => 'object', 'additionalProperties' => ['type' => 'string', 'format' => 'date']];
        $constraints['timestamps'] = ['type' => 'array', 'items' => ['type' => 'string', 'format' => 'date-time']];
        $constraints['shapes'] = ['type' => 'array'];
        $constraints['tagsByKey'] = ['type' => 'object'];

        return $constraints;
    }

    /**
     * Straight-line hydration from a decoded body — machinery for `DtoDeserializer`, not an API to
     * call yourself.
     *
     * This method holds STRUCTURE only: which keys to look for, which are required, what to pass
     * the constructor, which presence flags to raise. Every decision ABOUT A VALUE — casting, the
     * OpenAPI field constraints, the wording of an error — stays in the deserializer and arrives
     * through `$cast`. That is deliberate: the general loop and this method cannot answer
     * differently about a value, because only one of them has an opinion.
     *
     * Emitted only for a schema this shape covers: every property a plain body field, no parameter
     * source, no default, no `readOnly`, no inheritance, no discriminator.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data
     * @param Closure(string, mixed, array<int, string>): mixed $cast casts, and appends any problem to $errors
     * @param array<int, string> $errors
     */
    public static function hydrateFast(array $data, Closure $cast, array &$errors): self
    {
        $datesGiven = array_key_exists('dates', $data);
        if (!$datesGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "dates" not found in request.';
        }
        $dates = $datesGiven ? $cast('dates', $data['dates'], $errors) : null;
        $shapesGiven = array_key_exists('shapes', $data);
        if (!$shapesGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "shapes" not found in request.';
        }
        $shapes = $shapesGiven ? $cast('shapes', $data['shapes'], $errors) : null;
        $datesByDayGiven = array_key_exists('datesByDay', $data);
        $datesByDay = $datesByDayGiven ? $cast('datesByDay', $data['datesByDay'], $errors) : null;
        $timestampsGiven = array_key_exists('timestamps', $data);
        $timestamps = $timestampsGiven ? $cast('timestamps', $data['timestamps'], $errors) : null;
        $tagsByKeyGiven = array_key_exists('tagsByKey', $data);
        $tagsByKey = $tagsByKeyGiven ? $cast('tagsByKey', $data['tagsByKey'], $errors) : null;

        if ($errors !== []) {
            throw new RuntimeException(implode("\n", array_unique(array_filter($errors))));
        }

        /** @var array $dates */
        /** @var array $shapes */
        $dto = new self(
            dates: $dates,
            shapes: $shapes,
            datesByDay: $datesByDayGiven ? $datesByDay : UnsetValue::UNSET,
            timestamps: $timestampsGiven ? $timestamps : UnsetValue::UNSET,
            tagsByKey: $tagsByKeyGiven ? $tagsByKey : UnsetValue::UNSET,
        );

        // No presence flags to raise here: the constructor already derives each one from whether its
        // argument is the sentinel, and for every property this method passes exactly what the
        // caller sent. Assigning them again was dead code in every generated class.
        return $dto;
    }
}

    /**
     * Straight-line hydration from a decoded body — machinery for `DtoDeserializer`, not an API to
     * call yourself.
     *
     * This method holds STRUCTURE only: which keys to look for, which are required, what to pass
     * the constructor, which presence flags to raise. Every decision ABOUT A VALUE — casting, the
     * OpenAPI field constraints, the wording of an error — stays in the deserializer and arrives
     * through `$cast`. That is deliberate: the general loop and this method cannot answer
     * differently about a value, because only one of them has an opinion.
     *
     * Emitted only for a schema this shape covers: every property a plain body field, no parameter
     * source, no default, no `readOnly`, no inheritance, no discriminator.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data
     * @param Closure(string, mixed, array<int, string>): mixed $cast casts, and appends any problem to $errors
     * @param array<int, string> $errors
     */
    public static function hydrateFast(array $data, Closure $cast, array &$errors): self
    {
        $fileGiven = array_key_exists('file', $data);
        if (!$fileGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "file" not found in request.';
        }
        $file = $fileGiven ? $cast('file', $data['file'], $errors) : null;
        $nameGiven = array_key_exists('name', $data);
        if (!$nameGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "name" not found in request.';
        }
        $name = $nameGiven ? $cast('name', $data['name'], $errors) : null;
        $surnameGiven = array_key_exists('surname', $data);
        if (!$surnameGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "surname" not found in request.';
        }
        $surname = $surnameGiven ? $cast('surname', $data['surname'], $errors) : null;

        if ($errors !== []) {
            throw new RuntimeException(implode("\n", array_unique(array_filter($errors))));
        }

        /** @var UploadedFile $file */
        /** @var string $name */
        /** @var string $surname */
        $dto = new self(
            file: $file,
            name: $name,
            surname: $surname,
        );

        // No presence flags to raise here: the constructor already derives each one from whether its
        // argument is the sentinel, and for every property this method passes exactly what the
        // caller sent. Assigning them again was dead code in every generated class.
        return $dto;
    }
}

    /**
     * Straight-line hydration from a decoded body — machinery for `DtoDeserializer`, not an API to
     * call yourself.
     *
     * This method holds STRUCTURE only: which keys to look for, which are required, what to pass
     * the constructor, which presence flags to raise. Every decision ABOUT A VALUE — casting, the
     * OpenAPI field constraints, the wording of an error — stays in the deserializer and arrives
     * through `$cast`. That is deliberate: the general loop and this method cannot answer
     * differently about a value, because only one of them has an opinion.
     *
     * Emitted only for a schema this shape covers: every property a plain body field, no parameter
     * source, no default, no `readOnly`, no inheritance, no discriminator.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data
     * @param Closure(string, mixed, array<int, string>): mixed $cast casts, and appends any problem to $errors
     * @param array<int, string> $errors
     */
    public static function hydrateFast(array $data, Closure $cast, array &$errors): self
    {
        $matrixGiven = array_key_exists('matrix', $data);
        if (!$matrixGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "matrix" not found in request.';
        }
        $matrix = $matrixGiven ? $cast('matrix', $data['matrix'], $errors) : null;
        $rowsOfMapsGiven = array_key_exists('rowsOfMaps', $data);
        if (!$rowsOfMapsGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "rowsOfMaps" not found in request.';
        }
        $rowsOfMaps = $rowsOfMapsGiven ? $cast('rowsOfMaps', $data['rowsOfMaps'], $errors) : null;
        $listsByKeyGiven = array_key_exists('listsByKey', $data);
        $listsByKey = $listsByKeyGiven ? $cast('listsByKey', $data['listsByKey'], $errors) : null;
        $mapsByKeyGiven = array_key_exists('mapsByKey', $data);
        $mapsByKey = $mapsByKeyGiven ? $cast('mapsByKey', $data['mapsByKey'], $errors) : null;
        $dateRowsGiven = array_key_exists('dateRows', $data);
        $dateRows = $dateRowsGiven ? $cast('dateRows', $data['dateRows'], $errors) : null;
        $numRowsGiven = array_key_exists('numRows', $data);
        $numRows = $numRowsGiven ? $cast('numRows', $data['numRows'], $errors) : null;
        $enumRowsGiven = array_key_exists('enumRows', $data);
        $enumRows = $enumRowsGiven ? $cast('enumRows', $data['enumRows'], $errors) : null;
        $tagRowsGiven = array_key_exists('tagRows', $data);
        $tagRows = $tagRowsGiven ? $cast('tagRows', $data['tagRows'], $errors) : null;

        if ($errors !== []) {
            throw new RuntimeException(implode("\n", array_unique(array_filter($errors))));
        }

        /** @var array $matrix */
        /** @var array $rowsOfMaps */
        $dto = new self(
            matrix: $matrix,
            rowsOfMaps: $rowsOfMaps,
            listsByKey: $listsByKeyGiven ? $listsByKey : UnsetValue::UNSET,
            mapsByKey: $mapsByKeyGiven ? $mapsByKey : UnsetValue::UNSET,
            dateRows: $dateRowsGiven ? $dateRows : UnsetValue::UNSET,
            numRows: $numRowsGiven ? $numRows : UnsetValue::UNSET,
            enumRows: $enumRowsGiven ? $enumRows : UnsetValue::UNSET,
            tagRows: $tagRowsGiven ? $tagRows : UnsetValue::UNSET,
        );

        // No presence flags to raise here: the constructor already derives each one from whether its
        // argument is the sentinel, and for every property this method passes exactly what the
        // caller sent. Assigning them again was dead code in every generated class.
        return $dto;
    }

        $dto = new self(
            id: $id,
            name: $name,
        );

        // Mark as being in request if it wasn't explicitly passed to constructor
        if (!$this->tagsInRequest) {
            $this->tagsInRequest = true;
        }
    }

    {
        return $this->user !== UnsetValue::UNSET ? $this->user : null;
    }

    {
        return false;
    }

    {
        return $this->userInPath;

        return $this->userInCookie;
    }

    {
        return $this->userInRequest;
    }

    public function getUserType(): ?Test2UserType
    {
        return $this->userType !== UnsetValue::UNSET ? $this->userType : null;
    }

    /**
     * Straight-line hydration from a decoded body — machinery for `DtoDeserializer`, not an API to
     * call yourself.
     *
     * This method holds STRUCTURE only: which keys to look for, which are required, what to pass
     * the constructor, which presence flags to raise. Every decision ABOUT A VALUE — casting, the
     * OpenAPI field constraints, the wording of an error — stays in the deserializer and arrives
     * through `$cast`. That is deliberate: the general loop and this method cannot answer
     * differently about a value, because only one of them has an opinion.
     *
     * Emitted only for a schema this shape covers: every property a plain body field, no parameter
     * source, no default, no `readOnly`, no inheritance, no discriminator.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data
     * @param Closure(string, mixed, array<int, string>): mixed $cast casts, and appends any problem to $errors
     * @param array<int, string> $errors
     */
    public static function hydrateFast(array $data, Closure $cast, array &$errors): self
    {
        $idGiven = array_key_exists('id', $data);
        if (!$idGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "id" not found in request.';
        }
        $id = $idGiven ? $cast('id', $data['id'], $errors) : null;
        $nameGiven = array_key_exists('name', $data);
        if (!$nameGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "name" not found in request.';
        }
        $name = $nameGiven ? $cast('name', $data['name'], $errors) : null;
        $petGiven = array_key_exists('pet', $data);
        if (!$petGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "pet" not found in request.';
        }
        $pet = $petGiven ? $cast('pet', $data['pet'], $errors) : null;
        $createdAtGiven = array_key_exists('createdAt', $data);
        if (!$createdAtGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "createdAt" not found in request.';
        }
        $createdAt = $createdAtGiven ? $cast('createdAt', $data['createdAt'], $errors) : null;

        if ($errors !== []) {
            throw new RuntimeException(implode("\n", array_unique(array_filter($errors))));
        }

        /** @var int $id */
        /** @var string $name */
        /** @var ?Animal2 $pet */
        /** @var DateTimeImmutable $createdAt */
        $dto = new self(
            id: $id,
            name: $name,
            pet: $pet,
            createdAt: $createdAt,
        );

        // No presence flags to raise here: the constructor already derives each one from whether its
        // argument is the sentinel, and for every property this method passes exactly what the
        // caller sent. Assigning them again was dead code in every generated class.
        return $dto;
    }
}

     * The WIRE form, and the one place that knows this DTO is a `type: object`: an object with no
     * value to write is `{}`, never `[]`. PHP cannot tell those apart — both are the empty array —
     * so the empty case is cast here, where the schema is still known. `toArray()` keeps the honest
     * PHP view and stays an array.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|object
     */
    public function jsonSerialize(): array|object
    {
        $result = $this->toArray();

        return $result === [] ? (object)$result : $result;
    }

    /**
     * @throws JsonException
     */
    public function toJson(): string
    {
        return json_encode($this->jsonSerialize(), JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
    }

    public function __toString(): string
    {
        $json = json_encode($this->jsonSerialize(), JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE);

        return is_string($json) ? $json : '{}';
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    public static function getAliases(): array
    {
        return [];
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public static function getConstraints(): array
    {
        $constraints = [];
        $constraints['meow'] = ['type' => 'string'];
        $constraints['name'] = ['type' => 'string'];
        $constraints['bark'] = ['type' => 'string'];
        $constraints['test'] = ['type' => 'string'];

        return $constraints;
    }

    /**
     * Straight-line hydration from a decoded body — machinery for `DtoDeserializer`, not an API to
     * call yourself.
     *
     * This method holds STRUCTURE only: which keys to look for, which are required, what to pass
     * the constructor, which presence flags to raise. Every decision ABOUT A VALUE — casting, the
     * OpenAPI field constraints, the wording of an error — stays in the deserializer and arrives
     * through `$cast`. That is deliberate: the general loop and this method cannot answer
     * differently about a value, because only one of them has an opinion.
     *
     * Emitted only for a schema this shape covers: every property a plain body field, no parameter
     * source, no default, no `readOnly`, no inheritance, no discriminator.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data
     * @param Closure(string, mixed, array<int, string>): mixed $cast casts, and appends any problem to $errors
     * @param array<int, string> $errors
     */
    public static function hydrateFast(array $data, Closure $cast, array &$errors): self
    {
        $meowGiven = array_key_exists('meow', $data);
        if (!$meowGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "meow" not found in request.';
        }
        $meow = $meowGiven ? $cast('meow', $data['meow'], $errors) : null;
        $nameGiven = array_key_exists('name', $data);
        if (!$nameGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "name" not found in request.';
        }
        $name = $nameGiven ? $cast('name', $data['name'], $errors) : null;
        $barkGiven = array_key_exists('bark', $data);
        if (!$barkGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "bark" not found in request.';
        }
        $bark = $barkGiven ? $cast('bark', $data['bark'], $errors) : null;
        $testGiven = array_key_exists('test', $data);
        if (!$testGiven) {
            $errors[] = 'Required parameter "test" not found in request.';
        }
        $test = $testGiven ? $cast('test', $data['test'], $errors) : null;

        if ($errors !== []) {
            throw new RuntimeException(implode("\n", array_unique(array_filter($errors))));
        }

        /** @var string $meow */
        /** @var string $name */
        /** @var string $bark */
        /** @var string $test */
        $dto = new self(
            meow: $meow,
            name: $name,
            bark: $bark,
            test: $test,
        );

        // No presence flags to raise here: the constructor already derives each one from whether its
        // argument is the sentinel, and for every property this method passes exactly what the
        // caller sent. Assigning them again was dead code in every generated class.
        return $dto;
    }
}
